> 62	## 🎯 Validation plan → +3 BTC (testnet) — META NOVA (era 500k)
> 63	- **+3 BTC** = validar uma estratégia que cresce a carteira em 3 BTC (testnet); se valida 3, calibrar p/ 10+.
> 64	  No testnet (preços sintéticos) é **marco de mecânica/disciplina**, não alfa de mercado real.
> 65	- **Verdade dura do backtest (BTCUSDT 1h, net fee, bear 365d + bull 180d):** +3 BTC de **alfa absoluto** com
> 66	  long-only spot **NÃO sai de "trade esperto"** — bull→segurar ganha (B&H +134% vs regime +20%); bear→tudo perde
> 67	  mas o **caixa corta a perda** (regime −8% vs B&H −40%). Edge real = **drawdown menor via regime-gating**, não alfa.
> 68	  Calibração validada: RSI **25/75** (não 30/70), SMA **50/200** (não 20/50), menos trades. HFT é morto (fee+whipsaw).
> 69	  Rota p/ muitos BTC = **comprado no bull/trend, CAIXA no bear** + classificador bom + size pequeno + OCO. Doc:
> 70	  `~/STRATEGY-3BTC-assessment.md`. Painel `/intelligence` mostra o regime ao vivo (hoje BTC=bear/cash).
> 71	- (histórico 500k) = stress/validação, NÃO lucro (testnet fills sintético; reset ~mensal — persistir métricas).
> 72	- **Backtest de ciclo completo** (1h real, fee 0.1%/lado, walk-forward OOS, BTC/ETH/BNB/SOL):
> 73	  - **Bear (365d)**: B&H médio **-24%**; long-only não escapa; o edge é o **estado de CAIXA** (no BTC,
> 74	    regime-switch -7% vs B&H -27%). Regime-switch bate B&H em 1/4.
> 75	  - **Bull (2023-10→2024-04)**: B&H **+294%** ESMAGA tudo (regime +39%, trend +147%); bate B&H em 0/4.
> 76	  - **Lição honesta**: Buy&Hold é dificílimo de bater em retorno BRUTO; o valor do trading ativo é
> 77	    **risco-ajustado** (drawdown), não outperformance. HFT é matematicamente morto (fee+whipsaw).
> 78	- **Próximo edge**: classificador com **histerese** que FICA no bull e SAI no bear (ciclo completo,
> 79	  risco-ajustado) — é o trabalho do agente. Rodar `backtest.js` em mais janelas/símbolos.

> 86	  `exchange.js`: `test:true` roteia o **ws-api de testnet** p/ o userData (senão autentica no mainnet); ordens
> 87	  via `binance.order(type,side,...)` (buy/sell 1.x quebram STOP via flags); `ohlc()`→`populateOHLC()`;

> 97	- **Logger por categoria** (memória nova): `logger('M:'+id)`→`logs/M<id>.log`, `logger('A:'+id)`→`logs/A<id>.log`,
> 98	  `'system'`→`system.log`. NÃO grepar só `system.log` p/ debug de monitor/automação (custou tempo no OCO).

> ```
> 1	# CLAUDE.md
> 2	
> 3	This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.
> 4	
> 5	## What this is
> 6	
> 7	TradeUX is a multi-coin trading bot for **Binance Spot** with a web UI. The decision
> 8	core is based on **Beholder 2.1** (LuizTools' course) and is packaged to deploy in one
> 9	command with Docker. Three milestones live in one monorepo:
> 10	
> 11	- **`backend/`** + **`frontend/`** — the running product (manual operation). Node/Express
> 12	  + Sequelize + WebSocket API, React UI served by nginx.
> 13	- **`tradeux-mcp/`** — an MCP server that exposes the backend's REST API as tools.
> 14	- **`tradeux-agent/`** — an LLM agent (Anthropic or OpenRouter) that drives the bot
> 15	  through `tradeux-mcp`, with guardrails and an evaluation journal.
> 16	
> 17	## Architecture (the big picture)
> 18	
> 19	### Backend — the Beholder "brain" (`backend/src/`)
> 20	The backend is more than a CRUD API; it runs a stateful trading engine. Entry point is
> 21	`server.js`, which boots everything in order: load default settings → init the Beholder
> 22	brain with active automations → start the scheduler → start Express → attach WebSocket →
> 23	init the exchange/event monitor. The moving parts:
> 24	
> 25	- **`beholder.js`** — the in-memory engine. Holds `MEMORY` (live market state per
> 26	  symbol/index, e.g. MINI_TICKER, BOOK) and `BRAIN` (compiled automations indexed by the
> 27	  memory keys they react to). `updateMemory()` is called on every market tick and returns
> 28	  the automations whose conditions just fired. This is mutable module-level state guarded
> 29	  by `LOCK_*` flags — treat it carefully; it is the hot path.
> 30	- **`app-em.js`** (event/exchange monitor) — subscribes to Binance streams via
> 31	  `utils/exchange.js`, feeds ticks into `beholder.updateMemory`, and broadcasts results
> 32	  to UI clients. `app-ws.js` is the WebSocket server (JWT-authenticated via `?token=` in
> 33	  the URL). `app.js` is the Express app; `agenda.js` runs scheduled automations.
> 34	- **Layering**: `routers/` → `controllers/` → `repositories/` (Sequelize) → `models/`.
> 35	  Most domain concepts come in this full stack: settings, symbols, orders, monitors,
> 36	  automations, orderTemplates, withdrawTemplates, plus beholder/exchange/logs. When adding
> 37	  a feature, follow this chain end to end.
> 38	- **`utils/`** — `exchange.js` (node-binance-api wrapper, the Binance boundary),
> 39	  `crypto.js` (AES-256 for API-key storage — needs `AES_KEY` exactly 32 chars),
> 40	  `indexes.js` (technical-indicator calc + memory index keys), plus notification channels
> 41	  (email, sms, telegram, push).
> 42	
> 43	### MCP server (`tradeux-mcp/src/`)
> 44	A thin **stdio** MCP server. `api.js` is an HTTP client to the backend that logs in once
> 45	(`/login`), caches the JWT, and re-auths on 401. `index.js` registers ~25 tools named
> 46	`tradeux_*` (read: `get_wallet`, `get_price`, `get_analysis`, `get_memory`,
> 47	`list_orders`…; write: `place_order`, `cancel_order`, `start_automation`,
> 48	`stop_automation`…). It needs `TRADEUX_API_URL`, `TRADEUX_EMAIL`, `TRADEUX_PASSWORD`.
> 49	
> 50	### Agent (`tradeux-agent/src/`)
> 51	`index.js` is the CLI (`--check` lists MCP tools and exits; `--once` runs one cycle;
> 52	no flag = loop every `TRADEUX_AGENT_INTERVAL`). `mcpClient.js` spawns `tradeux-mcp` over
> 53	stdio (default path `../../tradeux-mcp/src/index.js`) and converts MCP tools to the model
> 54	tool format. `agent.js` runs the decision loop: a stable `SYSTEM_PROMPT` holding the
> 55	trading persona + **guardrails**, then up to `MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS` of tool calls, recording
> 56	everything to the journal. `providers/` abstracts Anthropic vs OpenRouter (selected by
> 57	`TRADEUX_AGENT_PROVIDER`). **Writes are gated by `TRADEUX_ALLOW_TRADING` (default off).**
> 58	
> 59	## Deployment model (decided)
> 60	
> 61	- **Operational DB = MySQL in the bundled Docker stack** (source of truth for orders,
> 62	  settings, automations). Self-contained — `./setup.sh` brings up `db` + `backend` +
> 63	  `frontend` on an isolated network/volume. This is the default and the validated path.
> 64	- **Memory / RAG = Supabase pgvector**, in a dedicated `tradeux_memory` database — kept
> 65	  separate from the operational DB. (Agent memory layer is a later milestone.)
> 66	- The backend uses Sequelize and speaks **both MySQL and Postgres**; migrations are
> 67	  portable (CI proves migrate+seed on both). The Supabase override
> 68	  (`docker-compose.supabase.yml`) exists for running the operational DB on Postgres
> 69	  instead, but the chosen setup keeps operational on the bundled MySQL.
> 70	- **Production fronting**: behind CloudPanel/Cloudflare set `WEB_BIND=127.0.0.1` and a
> 71	  free `WEB_PORT` (e.g. 8090), and the reverse proxy **must enable WebSocket upgrade**
> 72	  (the `/ws` endpoint depends on it). Run as the site's own system user (e.g.
> 73	  `gcrux-tradeux`), not root. `REACT_APP_*` URLs are baked into the React build, so they
> 74	  must be set correctly **before** building the frontend image.
> 75	
> 76	> The backend **must be able to reach Binance** — run it where Binance's API is not
> 77	> geo-blocked. Switching the DB to Supabase does not change this.
> 78	
> 79	## Commands
> 80	
> 81	### Full stack (Docker)
> 82	```bash
> 83	./setup.sh            # one-command bootstrap: create .env, generate secrets, validate, up
> 84	make up               # = ./setup.sh
> 85	make logs / ps / restart / down
> 86	make nuke             # down -v — DELETES the database volume
> 87	make migrate / seed   # run sequelize migrate / seed inside the backend container
> 88	
> 89	# Supabase/Postgres as the operational DB (no local MySQL):
> 90	docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.supabase.yml up -d --build backend frontend
> 91	```
> 92	`setup.sh` auto-generates `JWT_SECRET` (32 bytes hex) and `AES_KEY` (16 bytes = 32 chars).
> 93	On first run it stops if required `.env` fields (DB passwords, panel login, Binance keys)
> 94	are blank — fill them and re-run. The backend container's `docker-entrypoint.sh` runs
> 95	`db:migrate` + `db:seed:all` (idempotent) on every boot.
> 96	
> 97	### Backend (host dev, `backend/`)
> 98	```bash
> 99	npm run dev           # nodemon + dotenv
> 100	npm start             # node -r dotenv/config ./src/server
> 101	npm run migratedb     # npx sequelize-cli db:migrate
> 102	npm run seeddb        # npx sequelize-cli db:seed:all
> 103	# Run a single migration: npx sequelize-cli db:migrate --to <YYYYMMDD-name.js>
> 104	# Undo last:             npx sequelize-cli db:migrate:undo
> 105	```
> 106	Sequelize config is `backend/config/` and reads `DB_*` from env. Seeders use
> 107	`seederStorage: 'sequelize'` so `db:seed:all` is idempotent. There are no unit tests; the
> 108	CI's "tests" are migrate+seed (both dialects) and the agent `--check` integration.
> 109	
> 110	### Frontend (`frontend/`) — Vite/React
> 111	```bash
> 112	npm run dev           # vite dev server
> 113	npm run build         # production bundle (what CI and the Docker image run)
> 114	npm run preview
> 115	```
> 116	
> 117	### MCP + agent
> 118	```bash
> 119	# tradeux-mcp/  — usually launched by the agent, not directly
> 120	npm start
> 121	
> 122	# tradeux-agent/
> 123	node src/index.js --check   # connect MCP, list tools, exit (no model key needed)
> 124	node src/index.js --once    # one decision cycle
> 125	node src/index.js           # loop
> 126	```
> 127	
> 128	## CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`)
> 129	Three jobs, all on Node 22, triggered on PRs and pushes to `main`/`master`:
> 130	1. **frontend** — `vite build` must compile.
> 131	2. **backend** — matrix `[mysql, postgres]`: `db:migrate` + `db:seed:all`, then re-run to
> 132	   prove idempotency. Keep migrations/seeds portable across **both** dialects.
> 133	3. **integration** — installs `tradeux-mcp` + `tradeux-agent` and runs the agent
> 134	   `--check` (validates the MCP bridge end to end, no backend or model key).
> 135	
> 136	## Conventions & gotchas
> 137	- **Secrets**: `AES_KEY` must be exactly 32 chars (AES-256) — `setup.sh` enforces it; do
> 138	  not hand-edit it to another length. API keys are AES-encrypted at rest via `crypto.js`.
> 139	- **Testnet first**: default `.env` points at Binance **mainnet (real money)**. For
> 140	  testing use `testnet.binance.vision` for the API and **`stream.testnet.binance.vision`**
> 141	  (note the `stream.` host) for the stream, and keep `TRADEUX_ALLOW_TRADING` off.
> 142	- **Frontend env is build-time**: `REACT_APP_API_URL`, `REACT_APP_WS_URL`,
> 143	  `REACT_APP_BWS_URL` are compiled into the bundle (passed as Docker build args). Changing
> 144	  them requires a frontend rebuild, not just a restart.
> 145	- **Two databases, one engine each**: backend talks to one operational DB (MySQL by
> 146	  default); when on Postgres set `DB_DIALECT=postgres` and `DB_SSL=true` for managed
> 147	  Postgres (Supabase). Do not point the backend at the `tradeux_memory` RAG database.
> 148	- **Comments and docs in this repo are in Portuguese (pt-BR)** — match that when editing.
> 149	- See `SECURITY.md` for the dependency/vulnerability posture before bumping deps.
> 150	
> 151	## Branching
> 152	Active development branch for the current task: `claude/github-project-review-setup-k2ELV`.
> 153	CI runs on `main`/`master`.
> 154	
> ```

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	'use strict';
> 2	/*
> 3	 * Classificador de regime de mercado do TradeUX — decide em que "modo" operar.
> 4	 *
> 5	 *   bear  (maioria das velas recentes < SMA200)   -> risk-off: ficar em CAIXA
> 6	 *   trend (ADX >= 25)                              -> seguir tendência
> 7	 *   range (ADX < 20)                               -> mean-reversion (RSI)
> 8	 *   mixed (20 <= ADX < 25, não-bear)               -> neutro (default: mean-reversion, baixa convicção)
> 9	 *
> 10	 * Mesma lógica validada no harness de backtest (backend/scripts/backtest.js). No backtest
> 11	 * 1h/1-ano (BTC/ETH/BNB/SOL) o estado de CAIXA foi o maior edge num ano de baixa.
> 12	 *
> 13	 * Uso como módulo:  const { classifyRegime } = require('./regimeClassifier');
> 14	 *                   classifyRegime(candles)  // candles: [{high,low,close}, ...] (antigo->novo)
> 15	 * Uso como CLI:     node src/utils/regimeClassifier.js BTCUSDT [1h] [days]
> 16	 */
> 17	const ti = require('technicalindicators');
> 18	
> 19	const DEFAULTS = { adxPeriod: 14, smaPeriod: 200, rsiPeriod: 14, lookback: 200, bearFrac: 0.6, trendAdx: 25, rangeAdx: 20 };
> 20	
> 21	function classifyRegime(candles, opts = {}) {
> 22	  const o = { ...DEFAULTS, ...opts };
> 23	  if (!Array.isArray(candles) || candles.length < o.smaPeriod + 5) {
> 24	    return { regime: 'mixed', strategy: 'mean-reversion', reason: `dados insuficientes (${candles ? candles.length : 0} velas; precisa > ${o.smaPeriod})`, signals: {} };
> 25	  }
> 26	  const high = candles.map(c => +c.high), low = candles.map(c => +c.low), close = candles.map(c => +c.close);
> 27	
> 28	  const adxArr = ti.adx({ high, low, close, period: o.adxPeriod });
> 29	  const adx = adxArr.length ? adxArr[adxArr.length - 1].adx : null;
> 30	  const sma = ti.sma({ values: close, period: o.smaPeriod });
> 31	  const sma200 = sma.length ? sma[sma.length - 1] : null;
> 32	  const rsiArr = ti.rsi({ values: close, period: o.rsiPeriod });
> 33	  const rsi = rsiArr.length ? rsiArr[rsiArr.length - 1] : null;
> 34	  const last = close[close.length - 1];
> 35	
> 36	  // fração das últimas `lookback` velas abaixo da SMA200 (sma alinhado ao fim da série)
> 37	  const lb = Math.min(o.lookback, sma.length);
> 38	  let below = 0;
> 39	  for (let k = 0; k < lb; k++) {
> 40	    const ci = close.length - 1 - k, si = sma.length - 1 - k;
> 41	    if (si >= 0 && close[ci] < sma[si]) below++;
> 42	  }
> 43	  const belowFrac = lb ? below / lb : 0;
> 44	
> 45	  let regime, strategy, reason;
> 46	  if (belowFrac > o.bearFrac) { regime = 'bear'; strategy = 'cash'; reason = `${(belowFrac * 100).toFixed(0)}% das últimas ${lb} velas < SMA200 (risk-off)`; }
> 47	  else if (adx != null && adx >= o.trendAdx) { regime = 'trend'; strategy = 'trend-follow'; reason = `ADX ${adx.toFixed(1)} >= ${o.trendAdx} (tendência forte)`; }
> 48	  else if (adx != null && adx < o.rangeAdx) { regime = 'range'; strategy = 'mean-reversion'; reason = `ADX ${adx.toFixed(1)} < ${o.rangeAdx} (lateral)`; }
> 49	  else { regime = 'mixed'; strategy = 'mean-reversion'; reason = `ADX ${adx != null ? adx.toFixed(1) : '?'} intermediário, sem viés claro`; }
> 50	
> 51	  return {
> 52	    regime, strategy, reason,
> 53	    signals: {

> 138	function invertCondition(memoryKey, conditions) {
> 139	    const conds = conditions.split(' && ');
> 140	    const condToInvert = conds.find(c => c.indexOf(memoryKey) !== -1 && c.indexOf('current') !== -1);
> 141	    if (!condToInvert) return false;
> 142	
> 143	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('>=') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('>=', '<').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 144	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('<=') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('<=', '>').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 145	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('>') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('>', '<').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 146	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('<') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('<', '>').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 147	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('!') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('!', '=').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 148	    if (condToInvert.indexOf('==') != -1) return condToInvert.replace('==', '!==').replace(/current/g, 'previous');
> 149	    return false;
> 150	}

> 164	function calcPrice(orderTemplate, symbol, isStopPrice) {
> 165	    const tickSize = parseFloat(symbol.tickSize);
> 166	    let newPrice, factor;
> 167	
> 168	    // OCO usa preços explícitos (TP em limitPrice, stop em stopPrice), igual aos tipos LIMIT.
> 169	    if (LIMIT_TYPES.includes(orderTemplate.type) || orderTemplate.type === 'OCO') {
> 170	        try {
> 171	            if (!isStopPrice) {
> 172	                if (parseFloat(orderTemplate.limitPrice)) return orderTemplate.limitPrice;
> 173	                newPrice = Function("MEMORY", "return " + getEval(orderTemplate.limitPrice))(MEMORY) * orderTemplate.limitPriceMultiplier;
> 174	            }
> 175	            else {
> 176	                if (parseFloat(orderTemplate.stopPrice)) return orderTemplate.stopPrice;
> 177	                newPrice = Function("MEMORY", "return " + getEval(orderTemplate.stopPrice))(MEMORY) * orderTemplate.stopPriceMultiplier;
> 178	            }
> 179	        }
> 180	        catch (err) {
> 181	            if (isStopPrice)
> 182	                throw new Error(`Error trying to calc Stop Price with params: ${orderTemplate.stopPrice} x ${orderTemplate.stopPriceMultiplier}. Error: ${err.message}`);
> 183	            else
> 184	                throw new Error(`Error trying to calc Limit Price with params: ${orderTemplate.limitPrice} x ${orderTemplate.limitPriceMultiplier}. Error: ${err.message}`);
> 185	        }
> 186	    }
> 187	    else {
> 188	        const memory = MEMORY[`${orderTemplate.symbol}:BOOK`];
> 189	        if (!memory)
> 190	            throw new Error(`Error trying to get market price. OTID: ${orderTemplate.id}, ${isStopPrice}. No Book.`);
> 191	
> 192	        newPrice = orderTemplate.side === 'BUY' ? memory.current.bestAsk : memory.current.bestBid;
> 193	        newPrice = isStopPrice ? newPrice * orderTemplate.stopPriceMultiplier : newPrice * orderTemplate.limitPriceMultiplier;
> 194	    }
> 195	
> 196	    factor = Math.floor(newPrice / tickSize);
> 197	    return (factor * tickSize).toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 198	}
> 199	
> 200	function calcQty(orderTemplate, price, symbol) {
> 201	    let asset;
> 202	
> 203	    if (orderTemplate.side === 'BUY') {
> 204	        asset = parseFloat(MEMORY[`${symbol.quote}:WALLET`]);
> 205	        if (!asset) throw new Error(`There is no ${symbol.quote} in your wallet to place a buy.`);
> 206	    }
> 207	    else {
> 208	        asset = parseFloat(MEMORY[`${symbol.base}:WALLET`]);
> 209	        if (!asset) throw new Error(`There is no ${symbol.base} in your wallet to place a sell.`);
> 210	    }
> 211	
> 212	    let qty = orderTemplate.quantity.replace(',', '.');
> 213	    if (parseFloat(qty)) return qty;
> 214	
> 215	    const multiplier = orderTemplate.quantityMultiplier;
> 216	    const stepSize = parseFloat(symbol.stepSize);
> 217	
> 218	    let newQty, factor;
> 219	    if (orderTemplate.quantity === 'MAX_WALLET') {
> 220	        if (orderTemplate.side === 'BUY')
> 221	            newQty = (parseFloat(asset) / parseFloat(price)) * (multiplier > 1 ? 1 : multiplier);
> 222	        else
> 223	            newQty = parseFloat(asset) * (multiplier > 1 ? 1 : multiplier);
> 224	    }
> 225	    else if (orderTemplate.quantity === 'MIN_NOTIONAL') {
> 226	        newQty = (parseFloat(symbol.minNotional) / parseFloat(price)) * (multiplier < 1 ? 1 : multiplier);
> 227	    }
> 228	    else if (orderTemplate.quantity === 'LAST_ORDER_QTY') {
> 229	        const lastOrder = MEMORY[`${orderTemplate.symbol}:LAST_ORDER`];
> 230	        if (!lastOrder)
> 231	            throw new Error(`There is no last order to use as qty reference for ${orderTemplate.symbol}.`);
> 232	
> 233	        newQty = parseFloat(lastOrder.quantity) * multiplier;
> 234	        if (orderTemplate.side === 'SELL' && newQty > asset) newQty = asset;
> 235	    }
> 236	
> 237	    factor = Math.floor(newQty / stepSize);
> 238	    return (factor * stepSize).toFixed(symbol.basePrecision);
> 239	}
> 240	
> 241	function hasEnoughAssets(symbol, order, price) {
> 242	    const qty = parseFloat(order.quantity);
> 243	    if (order.side === 'BUY')
> 244	        return parseFloat(MEMORY[`${symbol.quote}:WALLET`]) >= (price * qty);
> 245	    else
> 246	        return parseFloat(MEMORY[`${symbol.base}:WALLET`]) >= qty;
> 247	}
> 248	
> 249	function calcQuoteQty(orderTemplate, symbol) {
> 250	    if (orderTemplate.type !== 'MARKET' || parseFloat(orderTemplate.quantity))
> 251	        throw new Error(`Only MARKET orders can cal quote qty.`);
> 252	
> 253	    const multiplier = orderTemplate.quantityMultiplier;
> 254	
> 255	    if (orderTemplate.quantity === 'MAX_WALLET') {
> 256	        if (orderTemplate.side !== 'BUY') throw new Error(`Only MARKET BUY orders can calc quote qty with MAX_WALLET`);
> 257	
> 258	        const asset = MEMORY[`${symbol.quote}:WALLET`];
> 259	        if (!asset) throw new Error(`There is no ${symbol.quote} in your wallet to place a buy.`);
> 260	
> 261	        return (parseFloat(asset) * (multiplier > 1 ? 1 : multiplier)).toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 262	    }
> 263	    else if (orderTemplate.quantity === 'MIN_NOTIONAL') {
> 264	        return (parseFloat(symbol.minNotional) * (multiplier < 1 ? 1 : multiplier)).toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 265	    }
> 266	
> 267	    throw new Error(`Invalid order template quantity ${orderTemplate.quantity}`);
> 268	}

> 270	// OCO protetiva (One-Cancels-the-Other): TP via LIMIT_MAKER (perna "above") + SL via
> 271	// STOP_LOSS_LIMIT (perna "below"). Quando uma perna executa, a Binance cancela a outra.
> 272	// As 2 pernas são persistidas com o MESMO orderListId. Usa o endpoint novo
> 273	// v3/orderList/oco (ver utils/exchange.oco). v1: só SELL protetivo (TP acima, SL abaixo).
> 274	async function placeOco(settings, automation, orderTemplate, symbol) {
> 275	    const side = orderTemplate.side.toUpperCase();
> 276	    if (side !== 'SELL')
> 277	        throw new Error(`OCO no momento só suporta SELL protetivo (TP+SL). Side recebido: ${side}`);
> 278	
> 279	    // Anti-empilhamento: a proteção é "uma OCO por vez". Se já há uma OCO aberta desta
> 280	    // automação para o símbolo, não coloca outra — a automação re-arma sozinha quando a
> 281	    // anterior resolver (TP/SL) ou for cancelada. Sem isso, uma automação sem índice (que
> 282	    // dispara a cada tick) ou condições oscilando empilhariam OCOs e drenariam o saldo.
> 283	    if (automation.id) {
> 284	        const symbolUpper = orderTemplate.symbol.toUpperCase();
> 285	        const open = await getOpenOcoOrders(automation.id);
> 286	        if (open && open.some(o => o.symbol === symbolUpper)) {
> 287	            if (automation.logs) logger('A:' + automation.id, `OCO já aberta para ${symbolUpper}; re-disparo ignorado.`);
> 288	            return { type: 'success', text: `OCO already open for ${symbolUpper}, skipped.` };
> 289	        }
> 290	    }
> 291	
> 292	    // TP (perna LIMIT_MAKER, acima) = limitPrice × limitPriceMultiplier.
> 293	    const tpPrice = calcPrice(orderTemplate, symbol, false);
> 294	    if (!isFinite(tpPrice) || !parseFloat(tpPrice))
> 295	        throw new Error(`Erro no preço de TP da OCO, OTID ${orderTemplate.id}: ${tpPrice}`);
> 296	
> 297	    // Gatilho do stop (belowStopPrice) = stopPrice × stopPriceMultiplier.
> 298	    const stopTrigger = calcPrice(orderTemplate, symbol, true);
> 299	    if (!isFinite(stopTrigger) || !parseFloat(stopTrigger))
> 300	        throw new Error(`Erro no preço de stop da OCO, OTID ${orderTemplate.id}: ${stopTrigger}`);
> 301	
> 302	    // Preço-limite da perna de stop (belowPrice) = gatilho: para um SELL fica marketável
> 303	    // (limite <= gatilho), garantindo a execução quando o stop dispara.
> 304	    const stopLimit = stopTrigger;
> 305	
> 306	    const quantity = calcQty(orderTemplate, tpPrice, symbol);
> 307	    if (!isFinite(quantity) || !parseFloat(quantity))
> 308	        throw new Error(`Erro na quantidade da OCO, OTID ${orderTemplate.id}: ${quantity}`);
> 309	
> 310	    const order = { symbol: orderTemplate.symbol.toUpperCase(), side, quantity };
> 311	    if (!hasEnoughAssets(symbol, order, tpPrice))
> 312	        throw new Error(`Sem saldo suficiente para a OCO ${side} ${quantity} ${order.symbol}.`);
> 313	
> 314	    const options = {
> 315	        aboveType: 'LIMIT_MAKER',
> 316	        abovePrice: tpPrice,
> 317	        belowType: 'STOP_LOSS_LIMIT',
> 318	        belowPrice: stopLimit,
> 319	        belowStopPrice: stopTrigger,
> 320	        belowTimeInForce: 'GTC'
> 321	    };
> 322	
> 323	    let result;
> 324	    const exchange = require('./utils/exchange')(settings);
> 325	    try {
> 326	        result = await exchange.oco(side, order.symbol, quantity, options);
> 327	    } catch (err) {
> 328	        logger('A:' + automation.id, err.body ? err.body : err);
> 329	        logger('A:' + automation.id, { order, options });
> 330	        return { type: 'error', text: `OCO failed! ` + (err.body ? JSON.stringify(err.body) : err.message) };
> 331	    }
> 332	
> 333	    const orderListId = result.orderListId;
> 334	    const reports = Array.isArray(result.orderReports) ? result.orderReports : [];
> 335	
> 336	    // A OCO já está VIVA na corretora. Se a resposta vier anômala (≠2 pernas), cancela a
> 337	    // lista para não deixar ordem órfã não-rastreável e falha.
> 338	    if (reports.length !== 2) {
> 339	        try { if (orderListId) await exchange.cancelOrderList(order.symbol, orderListId); }
> 340	        catch (e) { logger('A:' + automation.id, e.body ? e.body : e); }
> 341	        return { type: 'error', text: `OCO retornou ${reports.length} pernas (esperado 2); lista #${orderListId} cancelada.` };
> 342	    }
> 343	
> 344	    // Persiste AMBAS as pernas com o mesmo orderListId (consistente com placeOrder). Se a
> 345	    // persistência falhar, a OCO ficaria viva mas órfã (fora do getOpenOcoOrders e do hook
> 346	    // de cancelamento) → cancela na corretora e falha, mantendo DB e exchange coerentes.
> 347	    try {
> 348	        for (const r of reports) {
> 349	            await insertOrder({
> 350	                automationId: automation.id,
> 351	                symbol: order.symbol,
> 352	                quantity: r.origQty || quantity,
> 353	                type: r.type,
> 354	                side: r.side || side,
> 355	                orderListId,
> 356	                limitPrice: r.price && parseFloat(r.price) ? `${r.price}` : null,
> 357	                stopPrice: r.stopPrice && parseFloat(r.stopPrice) ? `${r.stopPrice}` : null,
> 358	                icebergQty: null,
> 359	                orderId: r.orderId,
> 360	                clientOrderId: r.clientOrderId,
> 361	                transactTime: r.transactTime || result.transactionTime || Date.now(),
> 362	                status: r.status || 'NEW'
> 363	            });
> 364	        }
> 365	    } catch (err) {
> 366	        logger('A:' + automation.id, err);
> 367	        try { if (orderListId) await exchange.cancelOrderList(order.symbol, orderListId); }
> 368	        catch (e) { logger('A:' + automation.id, e.body ? e.body : e); }
> 369	        return { type: 'error', text: `Falha ao persistir a OCO #${orderListId}; lista cancelada na corretora.` };
> 370	    }
> 371	
> 372	    if (automation.logs)
> 373	        logger('A:' + automation.id, `OCO #${orderListId} ${side} ${order.symbol}: TP ${tpPrice} / STOP ${stopTrigger}->${stopLimit}, qty ${quantity}`);
> 374	
> 375	    return { type: 'success', text: `OCO ${side} ${order.symbol} placed (list #${orderListId})` };
> 376	}

> 378	async function placeOrder(settings, automation, action) {
> 379	
> 380	    if (!settings || !automation || !action)
> 381	        throw new Error(`All parameters are required to place an order.`);
> 382	
> 383	    if (!action.orderTemplateId)
> 384	        throw new Error(`There is no order template for '${automation.name}', action #${action.id}`);
> 385	
> 386	    const orderTemplate = action.orderTemplate ? { ...action.orderTemplate } : await orderTemplatesRepository.getOrderTemplate(action.orderTemplateId);
> 387	    if (orderTemplate.type === 'TRAILING_STOP') {
> 388	        orderTemplate.type = 'MARKET';
> 389	        orderTemplate.limitPrice = null;
> 390	        orderTemplate.stopPrice = null;
> 391	    }
> 392	
> 393	    const symbol = await getSymbol(orderTemplate.symbol);
> 394	
> 395	    // OCO tem fluxo próprio (2 pernas + orderListId): desvia antes de montar a ordem simples.
> 396	    if (orderTemplate.type.toUpperCase() === 'OCO')
> 397	        return placeOco(settings, automation, orderTemplate, symbol);
> 398	
> 399	    const order = {
> 400	        symbol: orderTemplate.symbol.toUpperCase(),
> 401	        side: orderTemplate.side.toUpperCase(),
> 402	        options: {
> 403	            type: orderTemplate.type.toUpperCase()
> 404	        }
> 405	    }
> 406	
> 407	    const isDynamicBuy = order.side === 'BUY' && ['MIN_NOTIONAL', 'MAX_WALLET'].includes(orderTemplate.quantity);
> 408	    if (order.options.type === 'MARKET'
> 409	        && (isDynamicBuy || orderTemplate.quantity === 'MIN_NOTIONAL')) {
> 410	        order.options.quoteOrderQty = calcQuoteQty(orderTemplate, symbol);
> 411	    } else {
> 412	        const price = calcPrice(orderTemplate, symbol, false);
> 413	
> 414	        if (!isFinite(price) || !price)
> 415	            throw new Error(`Error in calcPrice function, params: OTID ${orderTemplate.id}, $: ${price}, stop: false`);
> 416	
> 417	        if (LIMIT_TYPES.includes(order.options.type))
> 418	            order.limitPrice = price;
> 419	
> 420	        const quantity = calcQty(orderTemplate, price, symbol, false);
> 421	
> 422	        if (!isFinite(quantity) || !quantity)
> 423	            throw new Error(`Error in calcQty function, params: OTID ${orderTemplate.id}, $: ${price}, qty: ${quantity}`);
> 424	
> 425	        order.quantity = quantity;
> 426	
> 427	        if (STOP_TYPES.includes(order.options.type)) {
> 428	            const stopPrice = calcPrice(orderTemplate, symbol, true);

> 430	            if (!isFinite(stopPrice) || !stopPrice)
> 431	                throw new Error(`Error in calcPrice function, params: OTID ${orderTemplate.id}, $: ${stopPrice}, stop: true`);

> 433	            order.options.stopPrice = stopPrice;
> 434	        }

> 440	    let result;

> 450	        logger('A:' + automation.id, err.body ? err.body : err);
> 451	        logger('A:' + automation.id, order);

> 458	    else if (STOP_TYPES.includes(order.type))

> 461	    const savedOrder = await insertOrder({
> 462	        automationId: automation.id,

> 464	        quantity: order.quantity || result.executedQty,
> 465	        type: order.options.type,

> 467	        limitPrice: LIMIT_TYPES.includes(order.options.type) ? order.limitPrice : null,

> 483	async function gridEval(settings, automation) {
> 484	    automation.grids = automation.grids.sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
> 485	
> 486	    if (LOGS)
> 487	        logger('A:' + automation.id, `Beholder is in the GRID zone at ${automation.name}`);
> 488	
> 489	    for (let i = 0; i < automation.grids.length; i++) {
> 490	        const grid = automation.grids[i];
> 491	        if (!Function("MEMORY", "return " + grid.conditions)(MEMORY)) continue;
> 492	
> 493	        if (automation.logs)
> 494	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `Beholder evaluated a condition at ${automation.name} => ${grid.conditions}`);
> 495	
> 496	        automation.actions[0].orderTemplateId = grid.orderTemplateId;
> 497	
> 498	        const book = MEMORY[`${automation.symbol}:BOOK`];
> 499	        if (!book) return { type: 'error', text: `No book info for ${automation.symbol}` };
> 500	
> 501	        const result = await placeOrder(settings, automation, automation.actions[0]);
> 502	        if (automation.logs) await require('./utils/telegram')(settings, result.text);
> 503	        if (result.type === 'error') return result;
> 504	
> 505	        const transaction = await db.transaction();
> 506	        try {
> 507	            const orderTemplate = await orderTemplatesRepository.getOrderTemplate(grid.orderTemplateId);
> 508	            await generateGrids(automation, automation.grids.length + 1, orderTemplate.quantity, transaction);
> 509	            await transaction.commit();
> 510	        } catch (err) {
> 511	            await transaction.rollback();
> 512	            logger('A:' + automation.id, err);
> 513	            return { type: 'error', text: `Beholder can't generate grids for ${automation.name}. ERR: ${err.message}` };
> 514	        }
> 515	
> 516	        automation = await automationsRepository.getAutomation(automation.id);//pega limpo
> 517	        updateBrain(automation);
> 518	        return result;
> 519	    }
> 520	}

> 522	async function generateGrids(automation, levels, quantity, transaction) {
> 523	
> 524	    await gridsRepository.deleteGrids(automation.id, transaction);
> 525	
> 526	    const symbol = await getSymbol(automation.symbol);
> 527	    if (!symbol.tickSize) throw new Error(`Tick Size not found for this symbol`);
> 528	
> 529	    const tickSize = parseFloat(symbol.tickSize);
> 530	
> 531	    const conditionSplit = automation.conditions.split(' && ');
> 532	    const lowerLimit = parseFloat(conditionSplit[0].split('>')[1]);
> 533	    const upperLimit = parseFloat(conditionSplit[1].split('<')[1]);
> 534	    levels = parseInt(levels);
> 535	
> 536	    const priceLevel = (upperLimit - lowerLimit) / levels;
> 537	    const grids = [];
> 538	
> 539	    let buyOrderTemplate, sellOrderTemplate;
> 540	    const orderTemplates = await orderTemplatesRepository.getOrderTemplatesByGridName(automation.name);
> 541	
> 542	    if (orderTemplates && orderTemplates.length) {
> 543	        buyOrderTemplate = orderTemplates.find(ot => ot.side === 'BUY');
> 544	        if (buyOrderTemplate && buyOrderTemplate.quantity !== quantity) {
> 545	            buyOrderTemplate.quantity = quantity;
> 546	            await orderTemplatesRepository.updateOrderTemplate(buyOrderTemplate.id, buyOrderTemplate);
> 547	        }
> 548	
> 549	        sellOrderTemplate = orderTemplates.find(ot => ot.side === 'SELL');
> 550	        if (sellOrderTemplate && sellOrderTemplate.quantity !== quantity) {
> 551	            sellOrderTemplate.quantity = quantity;
> 552	            await orderTemplatesRepository.updateOrderTemplate(sellOrderTemplate.id, sellOrderTemplate);
> 553	        }
> 554	    }
> 555	
> 556	    if (!buyOrderTemplate)
> 557	        buyOrderTemplate = await orderTemplatesRepository.insertOrderTemplate({
> 558	            name: automation.name + ' BUY',
> 559	            symbol: automation.symbol,
> 560	            type: 'MARKET',
> 561	            side: 'BUY',
> 562	            limitPrice: null,
> 563	            limitPriceMultiplier: 1,
> 564	            stopPrice: null,
> 565	            stopPriceMultiplier: 1,
> 566	            quantity,
> 567	            quantityMultiplier: 1,
> 568	            icebergQty: null,
> 569	            icebergQtyMultiplier: 1
> 570	        }, transaction)
> 571	
> 572	    if (!sellOrderTemplate)
> 573	        sellOrderTemplate = await orderTemplatesRepository.insertOrderTemplate({
> 574	            name: automation.name + ' SELL',
> 575	            symbol: automation.symbol,
> 576	            type: 'MARKET',
> 577	            side: 'SELL',
> 578	            limitPrice: null,
> 579	            limitPriceMultiplier: 1,
> 580	            stopPrice: null,
> 581	            stopPriceMultiplier: 1,
> 582	            quantity,
> 583	            quantityMultiplier: 1,
> 584	            icebergQty: null,
> 585	            icebergQtyMultiplier: 1
> 586	        }, transaction)
> 587	
> 588	    const book = MEMORY[`${automation.symbol}:BOOK`];
> 589	    if (!book) throw new Error(`There is no book info for ${automation.symbol}`);
> 590	
> 591	    const currentPrice = parseFloat(book.current.bestAsk);
> 592	    const differences = [];
> 593	
> 594	    for (let i = 1; i <= levels; i++) {
> 595	        const priceFactor = Math.floor((lowerLimit + (priceLevel * i)) / tickSize);
> 596	        const targetPrice = priceFactor * tickSize;
> 597	        const targetPriceStr = targetPrice.toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 598	        differences.push(Math.abs(currentPrice - targetPrice));
> 599	
> 600	        if (targetPrice < currentPrice) { //se está abaixo da cotação, compra
> 601	            const previousLevel = targetPrice - priceLevel;
> 602	            const previousLevelStr = previousLevel.toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 603	            grids.push({
> 604	                automationId: automation.id,
> 605	                conditions: `MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].current.bestAsk<${targetPriceStr} && MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].previous.bestAsk>=${targetPriceStr} && MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].current.bestAsk>${previousLevelStr}`,
> 606	                orderTemplateId: buyOrderTemplate.id
> 607	            })
> 608	        }
> 609	        else {//se está acima da cotação, vende
> 610	            const nextLevel = targetPrice + priceLevel;
> 611	            const nextLevelStr = nextLevel.toFixed(symbol.quotePrecision);
> 612	            grids.push({
> 613	                automationId: automation.id,
> 614	                conditions: `MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].current.bestBid>${targetPriceStr} && MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].previous.bestBid<=${targetPriceStr} && MEMORY['${automation.symbol}:BOOK'].current.bestBid<${nextLevelStr}`,
> 615	                orderTemplateId: sellOrderTemplate.id
> 616	            })
> 617	        }
> 618	    }
> 619	
> 620	    const nearestGrid = differences.findIndex(d => d === Math.min(...differences));
> 621	    grids.splice(nearestGrid, 1);
> 622	
> 623	    return gridsRepository.insertGrids(grids, transaction);
> 624	}
> 625	
> 626	async function withdrawCrypto(settings, automation, action) {
> 627	    if (!settings || !automation || !action)
> 628	        throw new Error(`All parameters are required to place an order.`);
> 629	
> 630	    if (!action.withdrawTemplateId)
> 631	        throw new Error(`There is no withdraw template for '${automation.name}', action #${action.id}`);
> 632	
> 633	    const withdrawTemplate = await withdrawTemplatesRepository.getWithdrawTemplate(action.withdrawTemplateId);
> 634	
> 635	    let amount = parseFloat(withdrawTemplate.amount);
> 636	    if (!amount) {
> 637	        if (withdrawTemplate.amount === 'MAX_WALLET') {
> 638	            const available = MEMORY[`${withdrawTemplate.coin}:WALLET`];
> 639	            if (!available) throw new Error(`No available funds for this coin.`);
> 640	
> 641	            amount = available * (withdrawTemplate.amountMultiplier > 1 ? 1 : withdrawTemplate.amountMultiplier);
> 642	        }
> 643	        else if (withdrawTemplate.amount === 'LAST_ORDER_QTY') {
> 644	            const keys = searchMemory(new RegExp(`^((${withdrawTemplate.coin}.+|.+${withdrawTemplate.coin}):LAST_ORDER)$`));
> 645	            if (!keys || !keys.length) throw new Error(`No last order for this coin.`);
> 646	
> 647	            amount = keys[keys.length - 1].value.quantity * withdrawTemplate.amountMultiplier;
> 648	        }
> 649	    }
> 650	
> 651	    const exchange = require('./utils/exchange')(settings);
> 652	
> 653	    try {
> 654	        const result = await exchange.withdraw(withdrawTemplate.coin, amount, withdrawTemplate.address, withdrawTemplate.network, withdrawTemplate.addressTag);
> 655	
> 656	        if (automation.logs) logger('A:' + automation.id, `WITHDRAW`, withdrawTemplate);
> 657	
> 658	        return { type: 'success', text: `Withdraw #${result.id} realized successfully for ${withdrawTemplate.coin}` };
> 659	    } catch (err) {
> 660	        throw new Error(err.response ? JSON.stringify(err.response.data) : err.message);
> 661	    }
> 662	}

> 667	    return { text: `Telegram sent from automation '${automation.name}'`, type: 'success' };

> 670	async function trailingEval(settings, automation, action) {
> 671	    const isBuy = action.orderTemplate.side === 'BUY';
> 672	
> 673	    const book = MEMORY[`${automation.symbol}:BOOK`];
> 674	    if (!book) return { type: 'error', text: `No book info for ${automation.name}` };
> 675	
> 676	    const activationPrice = parseFloat(action.orderTemplate.limitPrice);
> 677	    const stopPrice = parseFloat(action.orderTemplate.stopPrice);
> 678	
> 679	    const currentPrice = isBuy ? book.current.bestAsk : book.current.bestBid;
> 680	    const previousPrice = isBuy ? book.previous.bestAsk : book.previous.bestBid;
> 681	
> 682	    const isPriceActivated = isBuy ? currentPrice <= activationPrice : currentPrice >= activationPrice;
> 683	
> 684	    if (!isPriceActivated) return false;
> 685	
> 686	    if (LOGS)
> 687	        logger('A:' + automation.id, `Beholder is in the Trailing zone at ${automation.name}`);
> 688	
> 689	    const isStopActivated = isBuy ? currentPrice >= stopPrice && previousPrice < stopPrice
> 690	        : currentPrice <= stopPrice && previousPrice > stopPrice;
> 691	
> 692	    if (isStopActivated) {
> 693	        if (automation.logs || LOGS)
> 694	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `Stop price activated at ${automation.name}`);
> 695	
> 696	        const results = await placeOrder(settings, automation, action);
> 697	
> 698	        //para executar apenas uma vez
> 699	        deleteBrain(automation);
> 700	
> 701	        automation.isActive = false;
> 702	        await automationsRepository.updateAutomation(automation.id, automation);
> 703	
> 704	        return results;
> 705	    }
> 706	
> 707	    const newStopPrice = isBuy ? currentPrice * (1 + (parseFloat(action.orderTemplate.stopPriceMultiplier) / 100))
> 708	        : currentPrice * (1 - (parseFloat(action.orderTemplate.stopPriceMultiplier) / 100));
> 709	
> 710	    if (!stopPrice || (isBuy && newStopPrice < stopPrice) || (!isBuy && newStopPrice > stopPrice)) {
> 711	        if (LOGS)
> 712	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `Stop price changed to ${newStopPrice} at ${automation.name}`);
> 713	
> 714	        action.orderTemplate.stopPrice = newStopPrice;
> 715	        await orderTemplatesRepository.updateOrderTemplate(action.orderTemplate.id, action.orderTemplate);
> 716	    }
> 717	}
> 718	
> 719	function doAction(settings, action, automation) {
> 720	
> 721	    try {
> 722	        switch (action.type) {
> 723	            case actionTypes.ALERT_EMAIL: return sendEmail(settings, automation);
> 724	            case actionTypes.ALERT_SMS: return sendSms(settings, automation);
> 725	            case actionTypes.ALERT_TELEGRAM: return sendTelegram(settings, automation);
> 726	            case actionTypes.ORDER: return placeOrder(settings, automation, action);
> 727	            case actionTypes.TRAILING: return trailingEval(settings, automation, action);
> 728	            case actionTypes.WITHDRAW: return withdrawCrypto(settings, automation, action);
> 729	            case actionTypes.GRID: return gridEval(settings, automation);
> 730	        }
> 731	    } catch (err) {
> 732	        if (automation.logs) {
> 733	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `${automation.name}:${action.type}`);
> 734	            logger('A:' + automation.id, err);
> 735	        }
> 736	        return { text: `Error at ${automation.name}: ${err.message}`, type: 'error' };
> 737	    }
> 738	}
> 739	
> 740	function shouldntInvert(automation, memoryKey) {
> 741	    //return true;//descomente para desabilitar 'double check' (teste de condição invertida)
> 742	    return ['GRID', 'TRAILING'].includes(automation.actions[0].type)
> 743	        || automation.schedule
> 744	        || memoryKey.indexOf(':LAST_ORDER') !== -1
> 745	        || memoryKey.indexOf(':LAST_CANDLE') !== -1
> 746	        || memoryKey.indexOf(':PREVIOUS_CANDLE') !== -1;
> 747	}
> 748	
> 749	async function evalDecision(memoryKey, automation) {
> 750	    if (!automation) return false;
> 751	
> 752	    try {
> 753	        const indexes = automation.indexes ? automation.indexes.split(',') : [];
> 754	
> 755	        if (indexes.length) {
> 756	            const isChecked = indexes.every(ix => MEMORY[ix] !== null && MEMORY[ix] !== undefined);
> 757	            if (!isChecked) return false;
> 758	
> 759	            const invertedCondition = shouldntInvert(automation, memoryKey) ? '' : invertCondition(memoryKey, automation.conditions);
> 760	            const evalCondition = automation.conditions + (invertedCondition ? ' && ' + invertedCondition : '');
> 761	
> 762	            if (LOGS) logger('A:' + automation.id, `Beholder trying to evaluate:\n${evalCondition}\n at ${automation.name}`);
> 763	
> 764	            const isValid = evalCondition ? Function("MEMORY", "return " + evalCondition)(MEMORY) : true;
> 765	            if (!isValid) return false;
> 766	        }
> 767	
> 768	        if (!automation.actions || !automation.actions.length) {
> 769	            if (LOGS || automation.logs) logger('A:' + automation.id, `No actions defined for automation ${automation.name}`);
> 770	            return false;
> 771	        }
> 772	
> 773	        if ((LOGS || automation.logs) && !['GRID', 'TRAILING'].includes(automation.actions[0].type))
> 774	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `Beholder evaluated a condition at automation: ${automation.name} => ${automation.conditions}`);
> 775	
> 776	        const settings = await getDefaultSettings();
> 777	        const results = [];
> 778	
> 779	        for (let i = 0; i < automation.actions.length; i++) {
> 780	            const action = automation.actions[i];
> 781	            const result = await doAction(settings, action, automation);
> 782	            if (!result || result.type === 'error') break;
> 783	
> 784	            results.push(result);
> 785	        }
> 786	
> 787	        if (automation.logs && results && results.length && results[0])
> 788	            logger('A:' + automation.id, `Automation ${automation.name} finished execution at ${new Date()}\nResults: ${JSON.stringify(results)}`);
> 789	
> 790	        return results.flat();
> 791	    } catch (err) {
> 792	        if (automation.logs) logger('A:' + automation.id, err);
> 793	        return { type: 'error', text: `Error at evalDecision for '${automation.name}': ${err}` };
> 794	    }
> 795	}
> 796	
> 797	async function testAutomations(memoryKey) {
> 798	
> 799	    const automations = findAutomations(memoryKey);
> 800	
> 801	    if (!automations || !automations.length || isLocked(automations.filter(a => a).map(a => a.id))) {
> 802	        if (LOGS) console.log(`Beholder has no automations for memoryKey: ${memoryKey} or the brain is locked!`);
> 803	        return false;
> 804	    }
> 805	
> 806	    setLocked(automations.map(a => a.id), true);
> 807	    let results;
> 808	
> 809	    try {
> 810	        const promises = automations.map(async (automation) => {
> 811	            let auto = { ...automation };
> 812	
> 813	            if (auto.symbol.startsWith('*')) {
> 814	                const symbol = memoryKey.split(':')[0];
> 815	                auto.indexes = auto.indexes.replaceAll(auto.symbol, symbol);
> 816	                auto.conditions = auto.conditions.replaceAll(auto.symbol, symbol);
> 817	                if (auto.actions) {
> 818	                    auto.actions.forEach(action => {
> 819	                        if (action.orderTemplate)
> 820	                            action.orderTemplate.symbol = symbol;
> 821	                    })
> 822	                }
> 823	                auto.symbol = symbol;
> 824	            }
> 825	
> 826	            return evalDecision(memoryKey, auto);
> 827	        });
> 828	
> 829	        results = await Promise.all(promises);
> 830	        if (Array.isArray(results) && results.length)
> 831	            results = results.flat().filter(r => r);
> 832	
> 833	        if (!results || (Array.isArray(results) && !results.length))
> 834	            return false;
> 835	        else
> 836	            return results;
> 837	    }
> 838	    finally {
> 839	        setTimeout(() => {
> 840	            setLocked(automations.map(a => a.id), false);
> 841	        }, results && results.length ? INTERVAL : 0)
> 842	    }
> 843	}

> 845	function parseMemoryKey(symbol, index, interval = null) {
> 846	    const indexKey = interval ? `${index}_${interval}` : index;
> 847	    return `${symbol}:${indexKey}`;
> 848	}
> 849	
> 850	async function updateMemory(symbol, index, interval, value, executeAutomations = true) {
> 851	
> 852	    if (value === undefined || value === null) return false;
> 853	    if (value.toJSON) value = value.toJSON();
> 854	    if (value.get) value = value.get({ plain: true });
> 855	
> 856	    if (LOCK_MEMORY) return false;
> 857	
> 858	    const memoryKey = parseMemoryKey(symbol, index, interval);
> 859	    MEMORY[memoryKey] = value;
> 860	
> 861	    if (LOGS) logger('beholder', `Beholder memory updated: ${memoryKey} => ${JSON.stringify(value)}, will exec autos? ${executeAutomations}`);
> 862	
> 863	    if (!executeAutomations) return false;
> 864	
> 865	    return testAutomations(memoryKey);
> 866	}
> 867	
> 868	function deleteMemory(symbol, index, interval) {
> 869	    try {
> 870	        const indexKey = interval ? `${index}_${interval}` : index;
> 871	        const memoryKey = `${symbol}:${indexKey}`;
> 872	        if (MEMORY[memoryKey] === undefined) return;
> 873	
> 874	        LOCK_MEMORY = true;
> 875	        delete MEMORY[memoryKey];
> 876	
> 877	        if (LOGS) logger('beholder', `Beholder memory delete: ${memoryKey}!`);
> 878	    } finally {
> 879	        LOCK_MEMORY = false;
> 880	    }
> 881	}
> 882	
> 883	function getMemory(symbol, index, interval) {
> 884	    if (symbol && index) {
> 885	        const indexKey = interval ? `${index}_${interval}` : index;
> 886	        const memoryKey = `${symbol}:${indexKey}`;
> 887	
> 888	        const result = MEMORY[memoryKey];
> 889	        return typeof result === 'object' ? { ...result } : result;
> 890	    }
> 891	
> 892	    return { ...MEMORY };
> 893	}

> 895	function getBrain() {
> 896	    return { ...BRAIN };
> 897	}
> 898	
> 899	function getBrainIndexes() {
> 900	    return { ...BRAIN_INDEX };
> 901	}
> 902	
> 903	function flattenObject(ob) {
> 904	    var toReturn = {};
> 905	
> 906	    for (var i in ob) {
> 907	        if (!ob.hasOwnProperty(i)) continue;
> 908	
> 909	        if ((typeof ob[i]) == 'object' && ob[i] !== null) {
> 910	            var flatObject = flattenObject(ob[i]);
> 911	            for (var x in flatObject) {
> 912	                if (!flatObject.hasOwnProperty(x)) continue;
> 913	
> 914	                toReturn[i + '.' + x] = flatObject[x];
> 915	            }
> 916	        } else {
> 917	            toReturn[i] = ob[i];
> 918	        }
> 919	    }
> 920	    return toReturn;
> 921	}
> 922	
> 923	function getEval(prop) {
> 924	    if (prop.indexOf('MEMORY') !== -1) return prop;
> 925	    if (prop.indexOf('.') === -1) return `MEMORY['${prop}']`;
> 926	
> 927	    const propSplit = prop.split('.');
> 928	    const memKey = propSplit[0];
> 929	    const memProp = prop.replace(memKey, '');
> 930	    return `MEMORY['${memKey}']${memProp}`;
> 931	}
> 932	
> 933	function getMemoryIndexes() {
> 934	    return Object.entries(flattenObject(MEMORY)).map(prop => {
> 935	        if (prop[0].indexOf('previous') !== -1 || prop[0].indexOf(':') === -1) return false;
> 936	        const propSplit = prop[0].split(':');
> 937	        return {
> 938	            symbol: propSplit[0],
> 939	            variable: propSplit[1].replace('.current', ''),
> 940	            eval: getEval(prop[0]),
> 941	            example: prop[1]
> 942	        }
> 943	    })
> 944	        .filter(ix => ix)
> 945	        .sort((a, b) => {
> 946	            if (a.variable < b.variable) return -1;
> 947	            if (a.variable > b.variable) return 1;
> 948	            return 0;
> 949	        })
> 950	}

> 952	const DOLLAR_COINS = ['USD', 'USDT', 'USDC', 'BUSD'];
> 953	
> 954	function getStableConversion(baseAsset, quoteAsset, baseQty) {

> ```

> 4	const HOSTS = ['api.binance.com', 'data-api.binance.vision', 'api.binance.us'];
> 5	const CACHE = new Map();
> 6	const TTL = 120e3;   // 2 min
> 7	const BARS = 300;    // velas recentes buscadas (>= SMA200 + folga)
> 8	const MIN_BARS = 205; // mínimo p/ classificar (smaPeriod 200 + 5)
> 9	
> 10	function getJSON(host, path) {
> 11	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
> 12	        const req = https.get({ host, path, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'tradeux' }, timeout: 8000 }, r => {
> 13	            let d = '';
> 14	            r.on('data', c => d += c);
> 15	            r.on('end', () => { try { resolve({ status: r.statusCode, body: JSON.parse(d) }); } catch (e) { resolve({ status: r.statusCode, body: null }); } });
> 16	        });
> 17	        req.on('error', reject);
> 18	        req.on('timeout', () => { req.destroy(); reject(new Error('timeout')); });
> 19	    });
> 20	}
> 21	
> 22	// GET /analysis/regime/:symbol?interval=1h
> 23	// Regime de mercado REAL (bear/trend/range/mixed) das ~300 velas mais recentes da MAINNET,
> 24	// independente do testnet (regime = estado real do mercado). Read-only, sem chaves. Cache 2 min.
> 25	// Dados insuficientes (< 205 velas) => regime:'unknown' + insufficient:true (nunca um 'mixed' falso).
> 26	async function getRegime(req, res, next) {
> 27	    const symbol = String(req.params.symbol || '').toUpperCase();
> 28	    const interval = String(req.query.interval || '1h');
> 29	    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{5,20}$/.test(symbol)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'simbolo invalido' });
> 30	    if (!/^[0-9]+[mhdw]$/.test(interval)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'interval invalido' });
> 31	
> 32	    const key = `${symbol}:${interval}`;
> 33	    const cached = CACHE.get(key);
> 34	    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.t < TTL) return res.json({ ...cached.data, cached: true });
> 35	
> 36	    let candles = null, used = null;
> 37	    for (const host of HOSTS) {
> 38	        const r = await getJSON(host, `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${interval}&limit=${BARS}`).catch(() => null);
> 39	        if (r && r.status === 200 && Array.isArray(r.body) && r.body.length > 0) {
> 40	            candles = r.body.map(c => ({ high: +c[2], low: +c[3], close: +c[4] }));
> 41	            used = host; break;
> 42	        }
> 43	    }
> 44	    if (!candles) return res.status(502).json({ error: 'klines indisponiveis na Binance', symbol });
> 45	    if (candles.length < MIN_BARS) {
> 46	        return res.json({ symbol, interval, candles: candles.length, source: used, regime: 'unknown', strategy: 'hold', insufficient: true, reason: `dados insuficientes (${candles.length} velas; precisa >= ${MIN_BARS})`, signals: {} });
> 47	    }
> 48	
> 49	    const data = { symbol, interval, candles: candles.length, source: used, ...classifyRegime(candles) };
> 50	    CACHE.set(key, { t: Date.now(), data });
> 51	    res.json(data);
> 52	}
> 53	
> 54	module.exports = { getRegime };
> 55	
> ```

> 6	let AGENDA = {};

> 9	
> 10	function init(automations) {

> 12	        AGENDA = {};
> 13	        automations.map(auto => {

> 15	                addSchedule(auto.get ? auto.get({ plain: true }) : auto);

> 17	        if (LOGS) logger('system', 'Beholder Agenda has started!');

> 23	function verifyCron(schedule) {
> 24	    return /^(@(annually|yearly|monthly|weekly|daily|hourly|reboot))|(@every (\d+(ns|us|µs|ms|s|m|h))+)|((((\d+,)+\d+|(\d+(\/|-)\d+)|\d+|\*) ?){5,7})$/.test(schedule);
> 25	}

> 27	async function runSchedule(id) {

> 31

> 33	            result = result.filter(r => r);
> 34	
> 35	        if (!verifyCron(automation.schedule))
> 36	            await automationsRepository.updateAutomation(id, { isActive: false });//só executa uma vez
> 37	
> 38	        if (LOGS || automation.logs) logger('A:' + id, `The Scheduled Automation #${id} has fired at ${new Date()}!\n${JSON.stringify(result)}`);

> 49	        job = nodeSchedule.scheduleJob(automation.schedule, () => {
> 50	            runSchedule(automation.id);
> 51	        });
> 52	    }
> 53	    else {

> 57	        });
> 58	    }
> 59	
> 60	    if (!job) throw new Error(`Cant schedule the job. Probably invalid date/cron. schedule: ${automation.schedule}`);

> 62	    AGENDA[automation.id] = job;
> 63	    if (LOGS || automation.logs) logger('A:' + automation.id, `The Scheduled Automation #${automation.id} (${automation.schedule}) was added to agenda at ${new Date()}!`);
> 64	}
> 65	
> 66	function cancelSchedule(id) {

> 78	        }
> 79	    })
> 80	}
> 81

> 264	                if (data.e === 'executionReport')
> 265	                    processExecutionData(monitorId, data, executionBroadcast);
> 266	                else if (data.e === 'balanceUpdate' || data.e === 'outboundAccountPosition')
> 267	                    processBalanceData(monitorId, balanceBroadcast, logs, data)
> 268	            },
> 269	            // 2º callback dedicado ao listStatus (OCO). O all_updates_callback acima também
> 270	            // recebe o listStatus, mas o tratamos SÓ aqui para não processar em duplicidade.
> 271	            listStatusData => processListStatusData(monitorId, listStatusData, executionBroadcast)
> 272	        )
> 273	
> 274	        logger('M:' + monitorId, 'User Data Monitor has started!');
> 275	    } catch (err) {
> 276	        logger('M:' + monitorId, 'User Data Monitor has NOT started!\n' + err.message);
> 277	    }
> 278	}
> 279	
> 280	async function processChartData(monitorId, symbol, indexes, interval, ohlc, logs) {
> 281	    if (typeof indexes === 'string') indexes = indexes.split(',');
> 282	    if (!indexes || !Array.isArray(indexes) || indexes.length === 0) return false;
> 283	
> 284	    const memoryKeys = [];
> 285	
> 286	    indexes.map(index => {
> 287	        const params = index.split('_');
> 288	        const indexName = params[0];
> 289	        params.splice(0, 1);
> 290	
> 291	        try {
> 292	            const calc = execCalc(indexName, ohlc, ...params);
> 293	            if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, `${index}_${interval} calculated: ${JSON.stringify(calc.current ? calc.current : calc)}`);
> 294	            beholder.updateMemory(symbol, index, interval, calc, false);
> 295	
> 296	            memoryKeys.push(beholder.parseMemoryKey(symbol, index, interval));
> 297	        } catch (err) {
> 298	            logger('M:' + monitorId, `Exchange Monitor => Can't calc the index ${index}:`);
> 299	            logger('M:' + monitorId, err);
> 300	        }
> 301	    });
> 302	
> 303	    return Promise.all(memoryKeys.map(async (key) => {
> 304	        return beholder.testAutomations(key);
> 305	    }))
> 306	}
> 307	
> 308	function startChartMonitor(monitorId, symbol, interval, indexes, broadcastLabel, logs) {
> 309	    if (!symbol) return new Error(`Can't start a Chart Monitor without a symbol.`);
> 310	    if (!exchange) return new Error('Exchange Monitor not initialized yet.');
> 311	
> 312	    exchange.chartStream(symbol, interval || '1m', async (ohlc) => {
> 313	        const lastCandle = {
> 314	            open: ohlc.open[ohlc.open.length - 1],
> 315	            close: ohlc.close[ohlc.close.length - 1],
> 316	            high: ohlc.high[ohlc.high.length - 1],
> 317	            low: ohlc.low[ohlc.low.length - 1],
> 318	            volume: ohlc.volume[ohlc.volume.length - 1],
> 319	        };
> 320	
> 321	        const previousCandle = {
> 322	            open: ohlc.open[ohlc.open.length - 2],
> 323	            close: ohlc.close[ohlc.close.length - 2],
> 324	            high: ohlc.high[ohlc.high.length - 2],
> 325	            low: ohlc.low[ohlc.low.length - 2],
> 326	            volume: ohlc.volume[ohlc.volume.length - 2],
> 327	        };
> 328	
> 329	        const previousPreviousCandle = {
> 330	            open: ohlc.open[ohlc.open.length - 3],
> 331	            close: ohlc.close[ohlc.close.length - 3],
> 332	            high: ohlc.high[ohlc.high.length - 3],
> 333	            low: ohlc.low[ohlc.low.length - 3],
> 334	            volume: ohlc.volume[ohlc.volume.length - 3],
> 335	        };
> 336	
> 337	        if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, lastCandle);
> 338	
> 339	        try {
> 340	            beholder.updateMemory(symbol, indexKeys.LAST_CANDLE, interval, { current: lastCandle, previous: previousCandle }, false);
> 341	            beholder.updateMemory(symbol, indexKeys.PREVIOUS_CANDLE, interval, { current: previousCandle, previous: previousPreviousCandle }, false);
> 342	
> 343	            if (broadcastLabel && WSS) sendMessage({ [broadcastLabel]: lastCandle });
> 344	
> 345	            let results = await processChartData(monitorId, symbol, indexes, interval, ohlc, logs);
> 346	
> 347	            if (results) {
> 348	                results.push(await beholder.testAutomations(beholder.parseMemoryKey(symbol, indexKeys.LAST_CANDLE, interval)));
> 349	                results.push(await beholder.testAutomations(beholder.parseMemoryKey(symbol, indexKeys.PREVIOUS_CANDLE, interval)));
> 350	
> 351	                if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, `chartStream Results: ${results}`);
> 352	                results.flat().map(r => sendMessage({ notification: r }));
> 353	            }
> 354	        } catch (err) {
> 355	            if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, err);
> 356	        }
> 357	    })
> 358	    logger('M:' + monitorId, `Chart Monitor has started for ${symbol}_${interval}!`);
> 359	}
> 360	
> 361	function stopChartMonitor(monitorId, symbol, interval, indexes, logs) {
> 362	    if (!symbol) return new Error(`Can't stop a Chart Monitor without a symbol.`);
> 363	    if (!exchange) return new Error('Exchange Monitor not initialized yet.');
> 364	    exchange.terminateChartStream(symbol, interval);
> 365	    if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, `Chart Monitor ${symbol}_${interval} stopped!`);
> 366	
> 367	    beholder.deleteMemory(symbol, indexKeys.LAST_CANDLE, interval);
> 368	    beholder.deleteMemory(symbol, indexKeys.PREVIOUS_CANDLE, interval);
> 369	
> 370	    if (indexes && Array.isArray(indexes))
> 371	        indexes.map(ix => beholder.deleteMemory(symbol, ix, interval));
> 372	}
> 373	
> 374	function stopTickerMonitor(monitorId, symbol, logs) {
> 375	    if (!symbol) return new Error(`Can't stop a Ticker Monitor without a symbol.`);
> 376	    if (!exchange) return new Error('Exchange Monitor not initialized yet.');
> 377	
> 378	    exchange.terminateTickerStream(symbol);
> 379	
> 380	    if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, `Ticker Monitor ${symbol} stopped!`);
> 381	
> 382	    beholder.deleteMemory(symbol, indexKeys.TICKER);
> 383	}
> 384	
> 385	function getLightTicker(data) {
> 386	    delete data.eventType;
> 387	    delete data.eventTime;
> 388	    delete data.symbol;
> 389	    delete data.openTime;
> 390	    delete data.closeTime;
> 391	    delete data.firstTradeId;
> 392	    delete data.lastTradeId;
> 393	    delete data.numTrades;
> 394	    delete data.closeQty;
> 395	    delete data.bestBidQty;
> 396	    delete data.bestAskQty;
> 397	
> 398	    data.quoteVolume = parseFloat(data.quoteVolume);
> 399	    data.volume = parseFloat(data.volume);
> 400	    data.priceChange = parseFloat(data.priceChange);
> 401	    data.percentChange = parseFloat(data.percentChange);
> 402	    data.averagePrice = parseFloat(data.averagePrice);
> 403	    data.prevClose = parseFloat(data.prevClose);
> 404	    data.high = parseFloat(data.high);
> 405	    data.low = parseFloat(data.low);
> 406	    data.open = parseFloat(data.open);
> 407	    data.close = parseFloat(data.close);
> 408	    data.bestBid = parseFloat(data.bestBid);
> 409	    data.bestAsk = parseFloat(data.bestAsk);
> 410	
> 411	    return data;
> 412	}
> 413	
> 414	async function startTickerMonitor(monitorId, symbol, broadcastLabel, logs) {
> 415	    if (!symbol) return new Error(`Can't start a Ticker Monitor without a symbol.`);
> 416	    if (!exchange) return new Error('Exchange Monitor not initialized yet.');
> 417	
> 418	    exchange.tickerStream(symbol, async (data) => {
> 419	        if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, data);
> 420	
> 421	        try {
> 422	            const ticker = getLightTicker({ ...data });
> 423	            const currentMemory = beholder.getMemory(symbol, indexKeys.TICKER);
> 424	
> 425	            const newMemory = {};
> 426	            newMemory.previous = currentMemory ? currentMemory.current : ticker;
> 427	            newMemory.current = ticker;
> 428	
> 429	            const results = await beholder.updateMemory(data.symbol, indexKeys.TICKER, null, newMemory);
> 430	            if (results) results.map(r => sendMessage({ notification: r }));
> 431	
> 432	            if (WSS && broadcastLabel) sendMessage({ [broadcastLabel]: data });
> 433	        }
> 434	        catch (err) {
> 435	            if (logs) logger('M:' + monitorId, err);
> 436	        }
> 437	    })
> 438	    logger('M:' + monitorId, `Ticker Monitor has started for ${symbol}`);
> 439	}
> 440	
> 441	async function sendMessage(json) {
> 442	    try {
> 443	        if (json.notification) {
> 444	            const settings = await getDefaultSettings();
> 445	            push.send(settings, json.notification.text, 'Beholder Notification', json.notification);
> 446	        }
> 447	    } catch (err) { }
> 448	
> 449	    return WSS.broadcast(json);
> 450	}
> 451	
> 452	async function init(settings, wssInstance, beholderInstance) {
> 453	    if (!settings || !beholderInstance) throw new Error(`You can't init the Exchange Monitor App without his settings. Check your database and/or startup code.`);
> 454	
> 455	    WSS = wssInstance;
> 456	    beholder = beholderInstance;
> 457	    exchange = require('./utils/exchange')(settings);
> 458	
> 459	    const monitors = await getActiveMonitors();
> 460	    monitors.map(m => {
> 461	        setTimeout(() => {
> 462	            switch (m.type) {
> 463	                case monitorTypes.MINI_TICKER:
> 464	                    return startMiniTickerMonitor(m.id, m.broadcastLabel, m.logs);
> 465	                case monitorTypes.BOOK:
> 466	                    return startBookMonitor(m.id, m.broadcastLabel, m.logs);
> 467	                case monitorTypes.USER_DATA: {
> 468	                    if (!settings.accessKey || !settings.secretKey) return;
> 469	                    return startUserDataMonitor(m.id, m.broadcastLabel, m.logs);
> 470	                }
> 471	                case monitorTypes.CANDLES:
> 472	                    return startChartMonitor(m.id, m.symbol, m.interval, m.indexes ? m.indexes.split(',') : [], m.broadcastLabel, m.logs);
> 473	                case monitorTypes.TICKER:
> 474	                    return startTickerMonitor(m.id, m.symbol, m.broadcastLabel, m.logs);
> 475	            }
> 476	        }, 250)//Binance only permits 5 commands / second
> 477	    })
> 478	
> 479	    const lastOrders = await ordersRepository.getLastFilledOrders();
> 480	    await Promise.all(lastOrders.map(async (order) => {
> 481	        const orderCopy = getLightOrder(order.get({ plain: true }));
> 482	        await beholder.updateMemory(order.symbol, indexKeys.LAST_ORDER, null, orderCopy, false);
> 483	    }))
> 484	
> 485	    logger('system', 'App Exchange Monitor is running!');
> 486	}
> 487	
> 488	module.exports = {
> 489	    init,
> 490	    startChartMonitor,
> 491	    stopChartMonitor,
> 492	    startTickerMonitor,
> 493	    stopTickerMonitor,
> 494	    sendMessage
> 495	}
> 496	
> ```

> 145	function execCalc(indexName, ohlc, ...params) {
> 146	    switch (indexName) {

> 212	function getInsideCandle(ohlc, last, bars) {
> 213	    let hasInsideCandle = ohlc.high[last] < ohlc.high[last - 1] && ohlc.low[last] > ohlc.low[last - 1];
> 214	    if (hasInsideCandle && bars > 1) {
> 215	        for (let i = 1; i < bars; i++)
> 216	            hasInsideCandle = hasInsideCandle && ohlc.high[last - i] < ohlc.high[last - i - 1] && ohlc.low[last - i] > ohlc.low[last - i - 1];
> 217	    }
> 218	    return hasInsideCandle;
> 219	}
> 220	
> 221	function insideCandle(ohlc, bars = 1) {
> 222	    const current = getInsideCandle(ohlc, ohlc.high.length - 1, bars);
> 223	    const previous = getInsideCandle(ohlc, ohlc.high.length - 2, bars);
> 224	    return { current, previous };
> 225	}

> 461	        close: ohlc.close,

> 464	    return {
> 465	        current: adxResult[adxResult.length - 1],
> 466	        previous: adxResult[adxResult.length - 2]
> 467	    }
> 468	}

> 492	        high: ohlc.high,
> 493	        low: ohlc.low,
> 494	        fastPeriod,

> 496	    })
> 497	    return {

> 499	        previous: aoResult[aoResult.length - 2]

> 538	function KST(closes, ROCPer1 = 10, ROCPer2 = 15, ROCPer3 = 20, ROCPer4 = 30, SMAROCPer1 = 10, SMAROCPer2 = 10, SMAROCPer3 = 10, SMAROCPer4 = 15, signal = 3) {

> 549	        return { current: false, previous: false };
> 550	
> 551	    const kstResult = technicalindicators.kst({
> 552	        values: closes,

> 563	    return {

> 565	        previous: kstResult[kstResult.length - 2]
> 566	    }
> 567	}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrux-tradeux/tradeux/backend/src/utils/regimeService.js",
  "content": "'use strict';\n/*\n * Servi\u00e7o de regime de mercado \u2014 fonte \u00daNICA de verdade para \"em que modo operar\".\n *\n * Busca ~300 velas recentes da MAINNET (estado real do mercado, independente do testnet),\n * classifica via regimeClassifier e memoiza por 2 min. \u00c9 consumido por:\n *   - analysisController.getRegime  (endpoint REST GET /analysis/regime/:symbol, p/ a UI)\n *   - regimeJob                     (job peri\u00f3dico que grava MEMORY['<sym>:REGIME'] p/ gate)\n *\n * NUNCA lan\u00e7a: sempre retorna um objeto. O chamador decide o que fazer com cada caso:\n *   sucesso     -> { ..., regime: 'bear'|'trend'|'range'|'mixed', strategy, reason, signals, cached? }\n *   poucos dados-> { ..., regime: 'unknown', insufficient: true }\n *   sem klines  -> { ..., regime: 'unknown', unavailable: true }   (endpoint mapeia p/ 502; job preserva MEMORY)\n */\nconst https = require('https');\nconst { classifyRegime } = require('./regimeClassifier');\n\nconst HOSTS = ['api.binance.com', 'data-api.binance.vision', 'api.binance.us'];\nconst CACHE = new Map();\nconst TTL = 120e3;   // 2 min\nconst BARS = 300;    // velas recentes buscadas (>= SMA200 + folga)\nconst MIN_BARS = 205; // m\u00ednimo p/ classificar (smaPeriod 200 + 5)\n\nfunction getJSON(host, path) {\n    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n        const req = https.get({ host, path, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'tradeux' }, timeout: 8000 }, r => {\n            let d = '';\n            r.on('data', c => d += c);\n            r.on('end', () => { try { resolve({ status: r.statusCode, body: JSON.parse(d) }); } catch (e) { resolve({ status: r.statusCode, body: null }); } });\n        });\n        req.on('error', reject);\n        req.on('timeout', () => { req.destroy(); reject(new Error('timeout')); });\n    });\n}\n\n// Regime de mercado REAL das ~300 velas mais recentes da MAINNET. Read-only, sem chaves.\n// Cache 2 min \u2014 s\u00f3 o resultado COMPLETO \u00e9 cacheado (insuficiente/indispon\u00edvel n\u00e3o, p/ re-tentar).\nasync function getRegime(symbol, interval = '1h') {\n    const key = `${symbol}:${interval}`;\n    const cached = CACHE.get(key);\n    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.t < TTL) return { ...cached.data, cached: true };\n\n    let candles = null, used = null;\n    for (const host of HOSTS) {\n        const r = await getJSON(host, `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${interval}&limit=${BARS}`).catch(() => null);\n        if (r && r.status === 200 && Array.isArray(r.body) && r.body.length > 0) {\n            candles = r.body.map(c => ({ high: +c[2], low: +c[3], close: +c[4] }));\n            used = host; break;\n        }\n    }\n    if (!candles)\n        return { symbol, interval, candles: 0, source: null, regime: 'unknown', strategy: 'hold', unavailable: true, reason: 'klines indisponiveis na Binance', signals: {} };\n    if (candles.length < MIN_BARS)\n        return { symbol, interval, candles: candles.length, source: used, regime: 'unknown', strategy: 'hold', insufficient: true, reason: `dados insuficientes (${candles.length} velas; precisa >= ${MIN_BARS})`, signals: {} };\n\n    const data = { symbol, interval, candles: candles.length, source: used, ...classifyRegime(candles) };\n    CACHE.set(key, { t: Date.now(), data });\n    return data;\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { getRegime, TTL, MIN_BARS, BARS };\n"
}
```

Read-only, sem chaves. Cache 2 min.\n// Dados insuficientes (< 205 velas) => regime:'unknown' + insufficient:true (nunca um 'mixed' falso).\n// A busca+classifica\u00e7\u00e3o+cache vivem em utils/regimeService (compartilhado com o regimeJob).\nasync function getRegime(req, res, next) {\n    const symbol = String(req.params.symbol || '').toUpperCase();\n    const interval = String(req.query.interval || '1h');\n    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{5,20}$/.test(symbol)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'simbolo invalido' });\n    if (!/^[0-9]+[mhdw]$/.test(interval)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'interval invalido' });\n\n    try {\n        const data = await getRegimeData(symbol, interval);\n        if (data.unavailable) return res.status(502).json({ error: data.reason, symbol });\n        res.json(data);\n    } catch (err) {\n        next(err);\n    }\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { getRegime };\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrux-tradeux/tradeux/backend/src/regimeJob.js",
  "content": "'use strict';\n/*\n * Regime Job \u2014 grava o regime de mercado na MEMORY do Beholder periodicamente, para que as\n * automa\u00e7\u00f5es possam GATEAR a entrada (ex.: \"n\u00e3o comprar em bear\"). \u00c9 o ENABLER que torna a\n * estrat\u00e9gia regime-gated (a melhor risco-ajustada no backtest: caixa no bear) deploy\u00e1vel ao vivo.\n *\n * Escreve uma STRING simples por s\u00edmbolo:  MEMORY['<symbol>:REGIME'] = 'bear'|'trend'|'range'|'mixed'\n * para que a condi\u00e7\u00e3o da automa\u00e7\u00e3o seja uma igualdade trivial:  MEMORY['<symbol>:REGIME']!=='bear'.\n *\n * Decis\u00f5es de seguran\u00e7a (caminho de dinheiro):\n *  - executeAutomations=false: a escrita do regime N\u00c3O dispara ordens por si. O regime \u00e9 um GATE\n *    PASSIVO, lido quando o tick de pre\u00e7o/RSI dispara a automa\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 n\u00e3o um gatilho ativo (evita\n *    comprar sobre um sinal de RSI velho na cad\u00eancia do poll).\n *  - skip-on-unknown: se a Binance n\u00e3o responder ou houver poucos dados, N\u00c3O sobrescreve a MEMORY\n *    (preserva o \u00faltimo regime conhecido e evita derrubar o gate por um solu\u00e7o de rede). Combinado\n *    com 'BTCUSDT:REGIME' nos `indexes` da automa\u00e7\u00e3o, no 1\u00ba boot sem regime conhecido a automa\u00e7\u00e3o\n *    fica fail-safe (n\u00e3o compra at\u00e9 o regime ser conhecido).\n *  - kill-switch REGIME_JOB_ENABLED=false: para o job (e, com REGIME nos indexes, pausa as compras\n *    gateadas \u2014 fail-safe).\n *\n * Env:\n *  REGIME_JOB_ENABLED  (default 'true')   \u2014 liga/desliga o job\n *  REGIME_REFRESH_MS   (default 300000)   \u2014 intervalo do poll (5 min; regime de velas 1h muda devagar)\n *  REGIME_INTERVAL     (default '1h')     \u2014 timeframe das velas usadas na classifica\u00e7\u00e3o\n *  REGIME_SYMBOLS      (CSV, opcional)    \u2014 for\u00e7a a lista de s\u00edmbolos; sen\u00e3o usa os das automa\u00e7\u00f5es ativas\n */\nconst beholder = require('./beholder');\nconst automationsRepository = require('./repositories/automationsRepository');\nconst { getRegime } = require('./utils/regimeService');\nconst { indexKeys } = require('./utils/indexes');\nconst logger = require('./utils/logger');\n\nconst ENABLED = (process.env.REGIME_JOB_ENABLED || 'true') !== 'false';\nconst REFRESH_MS = parseInt(process.env.REGIME_REFRESH_MS || '300000');\nconst INTERVAL = process.env.REGIME_INTERVAL || '1h';\n\nlet timer = null;\nlet running = false;       // trava de re-entr\u00e2ncia: nunca roda dois ciclos sobrepostos\nconst LAST = {};           // \u00faltimo regime gravado por s\u00edmbolo (p/ logar s\u00f3 nas mudan\u00e7as)\n\n// S\u00edmbolos a monitorar: REGIME_SYMBOLS (CSV) se setado; sen\u00e3o os s\u00edmbolos n\u00e3o-wildcard das\n// automa\u00e7\u00f5es ATIVAS (assim novos s\u00edmbolos entram sozinhos). Wildcard ('*...') \u00e9 ignorado \u2014 n\u00e3o\n// d\u00e1 p/ saber o s\u00edmbolo concreto sem a memoryKey; use REGIME_SYMBOLS p/ esses casos.\nasync function resolveSymbols() {\n    const fromEnv = (process.env.REGIME_SYMBOLS || '').split(',').map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);\n    if (fromEnv.length) return [...new Set(fromEnv)];\n\n    try {\n        const autos = await automationsRepository.getActiveAutomations();\n        const syms = (autos || [])\n            .map(a => String((a.get ? a.get('symbol') : a.symbol) || '').toUpperCase())\n            .filter(s => s && !s.startsWith('*'));\n        return [...new Set(syms)];\n    } catch (err) {\n        logger('regime', `Falha ao resolver s\u00edmbolos das automa\u00e7\u00f5es: ${err.message}`);\n        return [];\n    }\n}\n\nasync function runOnce() {\n    if (running) return;\n    running = true;\n    try {\n        const symbols = await resolveSymbols();\n        if (!symbols.length) return;\n\n        for (const symbol of symbols) {\n            try {\n                const r = await getRegime(symbol, INTERVAL);\n                if (!r || !r.regime || r.regime === 'unknown') {\n                    logger('regime', `${symbol}: regime indefinido (${r ? r.reason : 'sem resposta'}); MEMORY preservada.`);\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                await beholder.updateMemory(symbol, indexKeys.REGIME, null, r.regime, false);\n\n                if (LAST[symbol] !== r.regime) {\n                    logger('regime', `${symbol}:REGIME => ${r.regime} (${r.reason})`);\n                    LAST[symbol] = r.regime;\n                }\n            } catch (err) {\n                logger('regime', `${symbol}: erro ao atualizar regime: ${err.message}`);\n            }\n        }\n    } finally {\n        running = false;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction start() {\n    if (!ENABLED) {\n        logger('regime', 'Regime job DESABILITADO (REGIME_JOB_ENABLED=false).');\n        return;\n    }\n    if (timer) return; // idempotente\n\n    runOnce(); // primeira passada imediata no boot (n\u00e3o espera o 1\u00ba intervalo)\n    timer = setInterval(runOnce, REFRESH_MS);\n    if (timer.unref) timer.unref(); // n\u00e3o segura o processo no shutdown\n    logger('regime', `Regime job iniciado (intervalo ${REFRESH_MS}ms, klines ${INTERVAL}).`);\n}\n\nfunction stop() {\n    if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }\n}\n\nfunction getStatus() {\n    return { enabled: ENABLED, refreshMs: REFRESH_MS, interval: INTERVAL, running, last: { ...LAST } };\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { start, stop, runOnce, getStatus };\n"
}
```
